CY2026-10-09#
party document Public statement

Τι άλλα στοιχεία χρειάζεται η κυβέρνηση για να πάρει ουσιαστικά μέτρα;

AKEL press spokesman Charis Polykarpou issued a written statement criticizing the government's insufficient measures against rising costs and demanding immediate policy interventions including targeted fuel subsidies, vehicle license fee reductions, market oversight, heating allowances expansion, permanent 5% VAT on electricity, and energy upgrade schemes with social criteria.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Τα στοιχεία που ανακοίνωσε, χθες, η Στατιστική Υπηρεσία σε σχέση με τις αυξήσεις σε τρόφιμα και βασικά αγαθά τεκμηριώνουν ότι η ακρίβεια έχει γονατίσει νοικοκυριά και μικρομεσαίες επιχειρήσεις.
The data announced yesterday by the Statistical Service regarding increases in food and basic goods document that high prices have brought households and small and medium-sized enterprises to their knees.
Χάρης Πολυκάρπου
Η κυβέρνηση του Νίκου Χριστοδουλίδη πρέπει να αντιληφθεί ότι σε μια οικονομία που ευημερεί δεν αρκεί μόνο να καταγράφονται δημοσιονομικά πλεονάσματα.
The government of Nikos Christodoulidis must understand that in a prosperous economy it is not enough to merely record fiscal surpluses.
Χάρης Πολυκάρπου

CY2026-10-08#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Το ΑΚΕΛ δίπλα στο Χαμπή Τσαγκάρη | Να διαφυλαχτεί ο πολιτιστικός πλούτος της Κύπρου

AKEL expressed support for Hambis Tsangaris and called for an immediate solution to ensure the continued operation of the Printmaking School and Museum in Platanisteia, after initiating contact with the Minister of Interior who committed to actions for resolution.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Ο Χαμπής, μία από τις σπουδαιότερες προσωπικότητες του πολιτισμού που έχει αναδείξει η Κύπρος, μέσα από τις δεκαετίες λειτουργίας της Σχολής και του Μουσείου, έχει συμβάλει καθοριστικά στην αποκέντρωση της πολιτιστικής δημιουργίας, στην ανάδειξη της υπαίθρου και στην προαγωγή της πλούσιας πολιτιστικής μας κληρονομιάς.
Hambis, one of the most important cultural figures that Cyprus has produced, through the decades of operation of the School and Museum, has contributed decisively to the decentralization of cultural creation, the promotion of the countryside and the advancement of our rich cultural heritage.
AKEL
η Πολιτεία έχει καθήκον να διαφυλάξει τόσο το έργο που επιτελείται, όσο και τον πολιτιστικό «θησαυρό» που φιλοξενείται στις εγκαταστάσεις στην Πλατανίστεια.
The State has a duty to safeguard both the work being carried out and the cultural 'treasure' housed in the facilities in Platanisteia.
AKEL

CY2026-10-07#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Όχι σε άλλες κεραίες στο Ακρωτήρι

AKEL MP Marina Nikolaou opposed the installation of new British military antennas in Akrotiri after a Parliamentary Environment Committee session, calling for scientific studies on environmental and health impacts and reiterating the party's position for abolition of British bases in Cyprus.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Το ΑΚΕΛ ανέκαθεν υπογράμμιζε ότι η παρουσία στρατιωτικών βάσεων στην επικράτεια της Κυπριακής Δημοκρατίας, είναι κακό κατάλοιπο της αποικιοκρατίας και πρέπει να καταργηθούν.
AKEL has always emphasized that the presence of military bases on the territory of the Republic of Cyprus is a bad remnant of colonialism and they must be abolished.
Marina Nikolaou
Ζητήσαμε να εκπονηθούν επιστημονικές και περιβαλλοντικές μελέτες, οι οποίες θα εξετάζουν όχι μόνο τις επιπτώσεις κάθε νέας εγκατάστασης ξεχωριστά, αλλά και τις σωρευτικές επιπτώσεις του συνόλου των υφιστάμενων και νέων υποδομών.
We requested that scientific and environmental studies be conducted, which will examine not only the impacts of each new installation separately, but also the cumulative impacts of all existing and new infrastructure.
Marina Nikolaou

Έλλειψη πολιτικής βούλησης για το πρόβλημα των σχολικών υποδομών

AKEL MP Christos Christofidis criticized the government after a Parliamentary Education Committee session for inadequate school infrastructure funding, noting that the 2027 Technical Services budget is lower than 2010 levels despite doubled construction costs, and called for urgent action on safety certifications, temporary classrooms, and sports facilities.

We have for 2027 an amount in the budget for Technical Services that is somewhat smaller than the amount we had in 2010.
Χρίστος Χριστοφίδης
Αυτή η εικόνα δεν έπεσε από τον ουρανό, είναι αποτέλεσμα πολιτικών αποφάσεων.
This picture did not fall from the sky, it is the result of political decisions.
Χρίστος Χριστοφίδης

CY2026-10-07#
party document Public statement

Η κυβέρνηση να γίνει προστάτης των συμφερόντων της κοινωνίας, όχι δικηγόρος των εταιρειών καυσίμων

AKEL spokesperson Charis Polykarpou criticized the Christodoulides government for failing to control fuel prices and demanded immediate measures including a request to the EU to abolish double taxation on fuel, targeted mobility subsidies, and reduced circulation fees.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Είναι ένα πράγμα η κυβέρνηση Νίκου Χριστοδουλίδη να χαρακτηρίζεται από αδυναμία για αποτελεσματική αντιμετώπιση της ακρίβειας, είναι πολύ χειρότερο να προκαλεί συνειδητά την κοινωνία, δίνοντας άφεση αμαρτιών στις εταιρείες καυσίμων στην Κύπρο.
It is one thing for the Nikos Christodoulides government to be characterized by an inability to effectively address high prices, it is much worse to consciously provoke society by giving absolution to fuel companies in Cyprus.
Χάρης Πολυκάρπου
Χρειάζεται η κυβέρνηση να προχωρήσει σε υποβολή αιτήματος στην Ευρωπαϊκή Ένωση για κατάργηση της διπλής φορολογίας στα καύσιμα και μαζί να λάβει έκτακτα μέτρα στήριξης της κοινωνίας που περιλαμβάνουν την εισαγωγή στοχευμένου επιδόματος μετακίνησης και μείωση των αδειών κυκλοφορίας
The government needs to proceed with submitting a request to the European Union for the abolition of double taxation on fuel and together take emergency support measures for society that include the introduction of a targeted mobility allowance and reduction of circulation fees
Χάρης Πολυκάρπου

CY2026-10-07#
party document Public statement

Σχέδιο Αειφόρου Ανάπτυξης Ακάμα: Ένα ακόμα μνημείο αποτυχίας της διακυβέρνησης Ν. Χριστοδουλίδη

AKEL criticizes the Christodoulidis government over failures in planning and implementing the Akamas National Forest Park Sustainable Development Plan, citing an Audit Office report documenting environmental damage, cost overruns, and potential criminal conduct that may be referred to the Attorney General.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Η αποτυχία της κυβέρνησης στην προστασία του Ακάμα αποτυπώνεται από την απουσία επαρκούς προληπτικού σχεδιασμού και ελέγχων, αδυναμία οι περιβαλλοντικοί όροι να ενσωματωθούν σωστά στα σχέδια, απόκλιση των κατασκευών σε αρκετές περιπτώσεις από τους όρους/σχέδια και εκ των υστέρων αντιμετώπιση προβλημάτων που προκάλεσαν περιβαλλοντική επιβάρυνση, καθυστερήσεις και σπατάλη δημόσιων πόρων.
The government's failure to protect Akamas is reflected in the absence of adequate preventive planning and controls, inability to properly integrate environmental conditions into the plans, deviation of constructions in several cases from the terms/plans, and retrospective handling of problems that caused environmental burden, delays and waste of public resources.
Progressive Party of Working People
Ο Ακάμας είναι κοινή κληρονομιά ολόκληρης της κυπριακής κοινωνίας, χρειάζεται προστασία στην πράξη, ορθή αξιοποίηση του δημόσιου χρήματος και έργα που υπηρετούν το περιβάλλον και την κοινωνία.
Akamas is the common heritage of the entire Cypriot society, it needs protection in practice, proper utilization of public money and projects that serve the environment and society.
Progressive Party of Working People

party document Public statement

11 Οκτωβρίου 1958: Ο Χαράλαμπος Φιλιππίδης πεθαίνει από βασανιστήρια των Άγγλων

The National Popular Front commemorated the death of Charalambos Filippidis on 11 October 1958, stating he died from torture by the British at the Paphos Auxiliary Corps Centre during the EOKA struggle.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Την επομένη κυκλοφόρησε η φήμη ότι δραπέτευσε και δεν τον ξαναείδαμε
The next day the rumor circulated that he escaped and we never saw him again
Georgios Giagkou-Tziortsis
Είναι βέβαιο ότι ο Χαράλαμπος Φιλιππίδης πέθανε από βασανιστήρια στο Κέντρο Επικουρικού Σώματος στην Πάφο, ωστόσο οι Άγγλοι ισχυρίστηκαν, μέσω επίσημου ανακοινωθέντος, ότι δραπέτευσε
It is certain that Charalambos Filippidis died from torture at the Auxiliary Corps Centre in Paphos, however the British claimed, through an official announcement, that he escaped
National Popular Front

CY2026-10-10#
party document Policy proposal

Δημογραφικό: ώρα μηδέν – Μεγαλύτερη από ποτέ η ανάγκη λήψης μέτρων

The National Popular Front (ELAM) issued a statement warning of demographic change in Cyprus due to declining births among Cypriot mothers and rising births among mothers from third countries, proposing legislative measures including revised child benefit criteria excluding third-country nationals with refugee status, financial support for Greek Cypriot families, and increased VAT thresholds for first homes.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Το κράτος καλείται να στηρίξει έμπρακτα τις κυπριακές οικογένειες, προτού γίνουμε μειονότητα στην ίδια μας την Πατρίδα.
The state is called upon to support Cypriot families in practice, before we become a minority in our own Homeland.
National Popular Front
Η επιβίωση του κυπριακού ελληνισμού αποτελεί υπαρξιακό ζήτημα και ως τέτοιο θα πρέπει να βρίσκεται στην κορυφή των προτεραιοτήτων της Πολιτείας.
The survival of Cypriot Hellenism is an existential issue and as such should be at the top of the State's priorities.
National Popular Front

CY2026-10-09#
party document Public statement

9 Οκτωβρίου 1958: Βασανίζεται μέχρι θανάτου ο Νικόλαος Γιάγκου

The National Popular Front commemorated the 9 October 1958 death of Nikolaos Giagkou, an EOKA member who died under torture by British and Turkish police officers without revealing the location of his comrades.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Ο ξυλοδαρμός από τους βασανιστές μας, που ήταν δύο αξιωματικοί της αστυνομίας, ένας Άγγλος και ένας Τούρκος και αρκετοί Άγγλοι στρατιώτες, συνεχιζόταν μια σε μένα, μια στον Νικολή, έξω από τη Μόρφου προς τη θάλασσα, κοντά σε ένα αυλάκι με νερό, όπου μας έριχναν, όταν λιποθυμούσαμε.
The beating by our torturers, who were two police officers, one English and one Turkish and several English soldiers, continued alternating between me and Nikolis, outside Morphou towards the sea, near a ditch with water, where they threw us when we fainted.
Antonis Papaevripidis
Σε μια στροφή του δρόμου ο Νικολής έγειρε πάνω στην πόρτα, η οποία άνοιξε, αφήνοντας το σώμα του να κρέμεται κατατσακισμένο, ταλαιπωρημένο, μη μπορώντας να κάμει την παραμικρή προσπάθεια, για να κρατηθεί.
At a turn in the road Nikolis leaned on the door, which opened, leaving his body hanging battered, tormented, unable to make the slightest effort to hold on.
Antonis Papaevripidis

party document Parliamentary intervention

Δηλώσεις Ε. Χαμπουλλά μετά τη συνεδρίαση της Επιτροπής Άμυνας

ELAM MP Evgenios Champoullas, chair of the Parliamentary Defence Committee, stated after a committee session that the state has failed to properly plan the location of National Guard training facilities and firing ranges, calling for immediate cooperation between the Defence and Interior Ministries to establish comprehensive zoning and safety regulations.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
δυστυχώς, έχουμε αποτύχει ως Πολιτεία να σχεδιάσουμε τη χωροθέτηση εγκαταστάσεων, οι οποίες είναι αναγκαίες για την εκπαίδευση και την επιχειρησιακή ικανότητα της Εθνικής Φρουράς
unfortunately, we have failed as a State to plan the location of facilities which are necessary for the training and operational capability of the National Guard
Evgenios Champoullas
είναι αδιανόητο να μην υπάρχει ζώνη προστασίας περιμετρικά των στρατοπέδων και κάποιοι, ασκώντας πιέσεις, να δημιουργούν εφαπτόμενες οικιστικές περιοχές και να ζητούν την απομάκρυνση είτε στρατοπέδων είτε Πεδίων Βολής
it is inconceivable that there is no protection zone around the perimeter of military camps and that some people, exerting pressure, create adjacent residential areas and demand the removal of either camps or firing ranges
Evgenios Champoullas

party document Public statement

Alice Weidel: Angehobene Herbstprojektion basiert auf hoher Staatsquote

AfD federal spokesperson Alice Weidel criticized the German government's raised economic growth forecast of 1.3 percent, arguing it is based on an increasing state quota financed by debt, and called for tax cuts, reduced bureaucracy, subsidy elimination, and opposition to higher contribution assessment limits planned for 2027.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die angehobene Herbstprojektion von Wirtschaftsministerin Reiche basiert auf einer immer weiter steigenden Staatsquote. Und der Staatskonsum erfolgt auf Pump. Die Schulden belasten nachfolgende Generationen.
The raised autumn projection by Economics Minister Reiche is based on an ever-increasing state quota. And state consumption is financed by debt. The debts burden future generations.
Alice Weidel
Merz' und Klingbeils Staat auf Pump ist jedenfalls brandgefährlich, aktuell vermeintlich positive Konjunkturdaten kaschieren lediglich den desolaten Zustand von Wirtschaft und Infrastruktur.
Merz's and Klingbeil's debt-financed state is in any case extremely dangerous; currently supposedly positive economic data merely conceal the desolate state of the economy and infrastructure.
Alice Weidel

Ce 8 octobre, grande mobilisation en soutien aux lycéen·nes et étudiant·es !

France Unbowed announces continued support and participation in youth mobilisations on 8 October and 13 October, demanding improved study conditions, staff recruitment, abolition of Parcoursup, and one-euro meals for all students.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

France Unbowed

France Unbowed

party document Policy proposal

Niche 2026 - En finir avec les amendes abusives

France Unbowed proposes to abolish fixed-penalty fines for criminal offenses, arguing they enable police arbitrariness and disproportionately affect precarious and racialized young men, creating debt spirals.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Le développement des amendes forfaitaires délictuelles ces dernières années accompagne la politique pénale répressive et autoritaire de la macronie.
The development of fixed-penalty fines for criminal offenses in recent years accompanies the repressive and authoritarian penal policy of Macronism.
France Unbowed
Nous proposons donc de supprimer les amendes forfaitaires délictuelles.
We therefore propose to abolish fixed-penalty fines for criminal offenses.
France Unbowed

FR2026-10-08#
party document Policy proposal

Niche 2026 - Défiscaliser les pensions alimentaires perçues

France Unbowed presents a bill to exempt child support payments received by custodial parents from taxation, index pensions to inflation, and strengthen family support allowances, addressing precarity of single-parent families.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Aujourd'hui, en plus d'être trop faible (200 euros en moyenne quand un enfant coûte 750 euros) la pension est déduite des revenus du parent qui la verse, mais imposée chez le parent qui la reçoit, dans l'écrasante majorité des cas, la mère.
Today, in addition to being too low (200 euros on average when a child costs 750 euros), the pension is deducted from the income of the parent who pays it, but taxed for the parent who receives it, in the overwhelming majority of cases, the mother.
France Unbowed
Lors de notre précédente niche, l'obstruction de l'extrême droite nous avait retardé l'examen du texte.
During our previous niche session, the obstruction of the far right had delayed our examination of the text.
France Unbowed

FR2026-10-08#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Niche 2026 - Moratoire sur le mégacanal Seine-nord

France Unbowed proposes a bill establishing a 10-year moratorium on the Seine-Nord Europe mega-canal project, citing ecological concerns including threats to agricultural land, wetlands, biodiversity, and water resources.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Notre proposition de loi instaure un moratoire de 10 ans sur le méga-canal Seine-Nord Europe, projet vieux de plus de 40 ans, en contradiction avec l'urgence écologique.
Our bill establishes a 10-year moratorium on the Seine-Nord Europe mega-canal, a project over 40 years old, in contradiction with the ecological emergency.
Anne Stambach-Terrenoir
Sur 107 kilomètres, ce chantier menace terres agricoles, zones humides, biodiversité et ressource en eau, avec 35 millions de m³ d'eau mobilisés, pour un coût pouvant dépasser 10 milliards d'euros.
Over 107 kilometers, this project threatens agricultural land, wetlands, biodiversity and water resources, with 35 million m³ of water mobilized, for a cost that could exceed 10 billion euros.
France Unbowed

FR2026-10-08#
party document Policy proposal

Niche 2026 - Rétablir le droit commun de la propriété en Outre-mer

France Unbowed proposes to repeal 2024 legislation that reduced the acquisitive prescription period to 10 years in overseas territories, arguing it exposes modest families to land dispossession by speculators and demanding a return to common law.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
En 2024, sans concertation, la prescription acquisitive a été ramenée à 10 ans en outre-mer (contre 30 ans ailleurs).
In 2024, without consultation, the acquisitive prescription was reduced to 10 years in overseas territories (compared to 30 years elsewhere).
France Unbowed
Faciliter les sorties d'indivision est nécessaire, mais pas au prix de la dépossession des plus fragiles. Nous demandons donc l'abrogation de ces deux mesures et le retour au droit commun.
Facilitating exits from joint ownership is necessary, but not at the cost of dispossessing the most vulnerable. We therefore demand the repeal of these two measures and a return to common law.
France Unbowed

Communist Party statement on the International Day for Palestine​

The Communist Party of Britain issued a statement on International Day for Palestine calling for implementation of UK arms bans announced in September 2026, maximum attendance at a national march, and continued boycott, divestment and sanctions against Israel while endorsing the two-state solution supported by Palestinian representatives.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
Despite sustained state repression, the mass movements for Palestinian freedom and sovereignty forced formal UK recognition of a Palestine state announced in September 2025 at the UN General Assembly, and in September 2026 long overdue bans on trade with illegal Israeli settlements on occupied Palestinian land and on arms sales which 'materially contribute' to the occupation.
Communist Party of Britain
Our task is to ensure these measures are implemented, since no UK arms contracts with Israel have been suspended since then.
Communist Party of Britain

NIKI party vice-president and MP Georgios Rountas attended a memorial ceremony for 1821 Greek Revolution hero Nikitaras in Chiliomodi, Corinthia, on 11 October 2026, laying a wreath and declaring that Greece must be liberated again in this critical era for the Greek nation.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

Σήμερα, σε αυτή την τόσο κρίσιμη εποχή για το έθνος των Ελλήνων, ευχόμαστε ολόψυχα ο καλός Θεός να γεμίσει τις καρδιές των Ελλήνων με το πύρωμα της ψυχής του μεγάλου αυτού ήρωα, του Νικηταρά, και να βοηθήσει να ελευθερωθεί και πάλι η Ελλάδα.
Today we honor the memory of this tremendous hero of the divinely inspired Revolution of 1821, Nikitas Stamatelopoulos, Nikitaras. Today, in this very critical era for the Greek nation, we wholeheartedly pray that the good God fills the hearts of Greeks with the fire of the soul of this great hero, Nikitaras, and helps Greece to be liberated once again.
Georgios Rountas
να παραδώσουμε στα παιδιά μας μια πατρίδα ελεύθερη, περήφανη και πιστή στις ιερές της παραδόσεις
to hand over to our children a homeland free, proud and faithful to its sacred traditions
NIKI

party document Parliamentary intervention

Κουρουπάκη: Σχέδια εγκατάστασης μπαταριών λιθίου κοντά σε οικισμούς της Αρκαδίας – «Κάτοικοι φοβούνται πυρκαγιές και έκλυση τοξικών αερίων»

NIKH MP Aspasia Kouroupaki submitted a parliamentary question to the Ministers of Environment and Energy and Climate Crisis and Civil Protection demanding full disclosure on licensing, fire risks, and operational readiness regarding planned lithium battery storage facilities near residential areas in Arcadia.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Κάτοικοι φοβούνται πυρκαγιές και έκλυση τοξικών αερίων
Residents fear fires and release of toxic gases
NIKH (article title)
Οι κάτοικοι της Αρκαδίας έχουν δικαίωμα να γνωρίζουν τι σχεδιάζεται κοντά στα σπίτια και στις περιουσίες τους, ποιοι κίνδυνοι έχουν αξιολογηθεί και ποια ακριβώς μέτρα έχει προβλέψει η Πολιτεία για την προστασία της ζωής, της υγείας και του τόπου τους.
The residents of Arcadia have the right to know what is being planned near their homes and properties, what risks have been assessed and what exactly measures the State has foreseen for the protection of their lives, health and place.
Aspasia Kouroupaki

12 Οκτωβρίου 1944 – Η απελευθέρωση της Αθήνας και η αρχή του τέλους για την Κατοχή της Ελλάδας από τους Ναζί

Voice of Reason and MEP Afroditi Latinopoulou issued a statement commemorating the 82nd anniversary of Athens' liberation from Nazi occupation on 12 October 1944, criticizing leftist claims that the communist EAM liberated Athens and emphasizing five milestones of the 1940s including the suppression of communist insurgency in December 1944 and August 1949.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Δεν είναι λίγοι όμως, εκείνοι που αυτοαποκαλούμενοι ως "φιλειρηνιστές", προερχόμενοι ασφαλώς από τον χώρο της Αριστεράς, τους υπερτονίζουν τα τελευταία χρόνια την σημασία της 12ης Οκτωβρίου 1944, προπαγανδίζοντας αναληθώς ότι το κομμουνιστικό Ε.Α.Μ. απελευθέρωσε την Αθήνα.
However, there are not few of those who call themselves 'pacifists', certainly coming from the Left, who in recent years overemphasize the significance of 12 October 1944, falsely propagandizing that the communist EAM liberated Athens.
Voice of Reason
Η ΦΩΝΗ ΛΟΓΙΚΗΣ και η Πρόεδρός της, Ευρωβουλευτής Αφροδίτη Λατινοπούλου, τιμούν, όπως και κάθε Έλληνας Πατριώτης τα 5 ορόσημα της Επικής Δεκαετίας του 1940
VOICE OF REASON and its President, MEP Afroditi Latinopoulou, honor, like every Greek Patriot, the 5 milestones of the Epic Decade of the 1940s
Voice of Reason

IE2026-10-09#
party document Public statement

People Power Forced Drumcree U-Turn

People Before Profit claims community mobilisation forced the Secretary of State to cancel a contested Orange Order march on Garvaghy Road, criticises the DUP and unionist elites for sectarian division, and calls for a socialist Ireland built by working-class unity across communities.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
This didn't come from the Parades Commission, which folded the moment the DUP and the TUV turned up the pressure. It didn't come from the courts, which made a series of harried decisions through the night. Nor did it come from a Secretary of State who spent nearly a fortnight disingenuously insisting his hands were tied. It came from the community of the Garvaghy Road, as well as thousands of ordinary people standing with them over the past two weeks.
People Before Profit
The DUP can't build houses. It can't get people a hospital appointment. It can't deliver a single thing for the communities it claims to speak for. It reaches for sectarian division instead, because division is the only thing it has ever reliably produced.
People Before Profit

IT2026-10-08#
party document Policy proposal

Elezioni: con approvazione legge popolo torna sovrano

Prime Minister Giorgia Meloni announces the definitive approval of a new electoral law that reintroduces preference voting after over thirty years, introduces out-of-residence voting for students and workers, and aims to ensure governmental stability.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con l'approvazione definitiva della nuova legge elettorale, quando gli italiani torneranno a votare potranno scegliere tutto: il partito, la coalizione, il programma, il candidato premier e – di nuovo – il proprio parlamentare.
With the definitive approval of the new electoral law, when Italians return to vote they will be able to choose everything: the party, the coalition, the program, the prime ministerial candidate and – again – their own parliamentarian.
Giorgia Meloni
Il centrodestra reintroduce il voto di preferenza, che mancava da oltre trent'anni, restituendo ai cittadini il potere di scegliere direttamente chi li rappresenta.
The center-right reintroduces preference voting, which had been missing for over thirty years, returning to citizens the power to directly choose who represents them.
Giorgia Meloni

party document Policy proposal

Sanità: aumenti fino a 560 euro per personale medico

Prime Minister Giorgia Meloni announced contract renewal for healthcare workers bringing average monthly increases of 393 euros for over 137,000 medical personnel, with potential total increases up to 560 euros.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Un altro passo concreto per valorizzare chi ogni giorno si prende cura della salute degli italiani. Con il rinnovo del contratto 2025-2027 dell'Area Sanità, che riguarda oltre 137 mila medici e dirigenti sanitari, arrivano aumenti medi di 393 euro al mese per 13 mensilità, ai quali possono aggiungersi ulteriori risorse previste dalla legge, fino a un incremento complessivo di circa 560 euro.
Another concrete step to value those who every day take care of the health of Italians. With the renewal of the 2025-2027 contract for the Health Area, which concerns over 137 thousand doctors and health managers, average increases of 393 euros per month for 13 months are coming, to which additional resources provided by law can be added, up to a total increase of about 560 euros.
Giorgia Meloni
Continuiamo a investire sulle professionalità del nostro Servizio sanitario nazionale, riconoscendo il lavoro, le competenze e le responsabilità di chi ogni giorno garantisce assistenza e cure ai cittadini.
We continue to invest in the professionalism of our National Health Service, recognizing the work, skills and responsibilities of those who every day guarantee assistance and care to citizens.
Giorgia Meloni

party document Policy proposal

Pesca: con ‘cambiale azzurra’ 10 milioni per liquidità imprese

Minister Francesco Lollobrigida announced the 'Cambiale Azzurra' program providing 10 million euros in zero-interest financing to fishing and aquaculture businesses, with loans up to 20,000-60,000 euros per enterprise, applications open October 13-30 via Ismea portal.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Grazie a la Cambiale Azzurra, un intervento che abbiamo previsto in queste ore, garantiremo liquidità alle imprese di pesca e acquacoltura. Thanks to the Cambiale Azzurra, an intervention we have planned in these hours, we will guarantee liquidity to fishing and aquaculture businesses. With an investment of 10 million euros, those admitted will be able to access financing up to 20,000 euros, for a maximum of 60,000 euros for businesses or shipowner cooperatives with multiple vessels.
Francesco Lollobrigida
Questo provvedimento rappresenta un altro tassello del lavoro che stiamo portando avanti per sostenere la pesca italiana: più liquidità, più strumenti, più sostegno alle imprese.
This measure represents another piece of the work we are carrying forward to support Italian fishing: more liquidity, more tools, more support for businesses.
Francesco Lollobrigida

IT2026-10-09#
party document Public statement

PCI: Melonellum, le ombre della legge Acerbo e i rischi per la democrazia. DIFENDIAMO LA COSTITUZIONE!

The Italian Communist Party condemns the proposed electoral law (dubbed 'Melonellum') as unconstitutional, drawing parallels to the 1923 Acerbo law that facilitated fascism, and pledges to defend the Constitution through political and legal challenges.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Le similitudini con la legge Acerbo del 1923 non possono essere liquidate come un semplice richiamo storico. Quella legge assegnava i due terzi dei seggi alla lista più votata che avesse superato il 25% dei voti validi, contribuendo a consegnare al fascismo una maggioranza parlamentare schiacciante dopo le elezioni del 1924.
The similarities with the Acerbo law of 1923 cannot be dismissed as a simple historical reference. That law assigned two-thirds of the seats to the most-voted list that exceeded 25% of valid votes, contributing to delivering to fascism an overwhelming parliamentary majority after the 1924 elections.
Italian Communist Party
Il PCI non accetterà che il pluralismo venga sacrificato sull'altare della governabilità o che la sovranità popolare venga svuotata da regole costruite a vantaggio di chi detiene già il potere.
The PCI will not accept that pluralism be sacrificed on the altar of governability or that popular sovereignty be emptied by rules constructed to advantage those who already hold power.
Italian Communist Party

NO2026-10-08#
party document Public statement

– Som å tro på julenissen

Progress Party leader Sylvi Listhaug criticizes the Labour Party government's budget, claiming tax cuts are offset by increased electricity price surcharges, and proposes FrP's alternative of tax cuts, halving food VAT, and reducing fuel taxes.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Å tro at Arbeiderpartiet kutter skatter og avgifter er som å tro på julenissen
To believe that the Labour Party cuts taxes and fees is like believing in Santa Claus
Sylvi Listhaug
Regjeringen har forsøkt å skape et inntrykk av at de gir folk skattelette, men øker avgifter og Norgespris på strøm som gjør at svært mange sitter igjen med mindre i lommeboka. Frp vil kutte skattene, halvere matmomsen og redusere drivstoffavgiftene for å gi folk mer økonomisk handlefrihet
The government has tried to create an impression that they are giving people tax relief, but increases fees and the Norway price on electricity which means that very many are left with less in their wallet. FrP will cut taxes, halve the food VAT and reduce fuel taxes to give people more economic freedom
Sylvi Listhaug
